# M7 — State-management refactor cleanup (7 work packages bundled)

## Context

The M1-M6 megaplan chain shipped state-management code that caused three browser-breaking regressions, all from the same anti-pattern: zustand stores mirror state already owned elsewhere (React Query, React Context, Base UI popup open-state), with sync bridges that feedback-loop.

Baseline commit 5fa6917c9 (plus a small follow-up lightbox keyboard-nav fix at 8c613d041) contains band-aid patches that unblock the app with diagnostic console logs. This milestone replaces the band-aids with proper fixes and removes the underlying mirror pattern at 7 sites. All 7 work packages are bundled here because several are trivial and several are interdependent.

Current branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup`, worktree at `/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`.

## Work packages (execute in order; each is a distinct unit with its own success criteria)

### WP1 — Delete panes context zombie

Grep audit showed `usePaneLockPolicyState` has no readers outside `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts` itself. The context is dead weight the M1-M6 refactor forgot to remove.

- Find the `usePaneLockPolicyState` definition: `rg -l 'function usePaneLockPolicyState\|export.*usePaneLockPolicyState' src`.
- Inline its body directly into `useBootstrapPanesStore` in `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`.
- Delete the source file, any tests that only covered it, and any mocks that imported from it.
- After: `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` returns nothing.

### WP2 — Strip diagnostic logs

Baseline added console.log/warn tagged `[PanesBoot]`, `[OverlayStack]`, `[AutoSave]`. Diagnosis is done.

- Enumerate: `rg '\[PanesBoot\]|\[OverlayStack\]|\[AutoSave\]' src --glob '!*.test.*'`.
- Delete every matched console.log/console.warn statement and any nearby object-literal helpers that exist only to build the log payload.
- Do NOT touch other console statements. Do NOT touch test files.
- After: the rg above returns nothing.

### WP3 — Overlay bridge requires explicit open prop (pre-check required)

The Select wrapper registers its popup with the overlay stack at ref attachment, regardless of whether the Select is open. Baseline patches this by gating `getTopModalOverlay` on DOM `data-state`/`data-open` attributes via `isOverlayActuallyOpen` in `src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`. This is a fragile workaround.

**Pre-check before coding**: verify Base UI exposes the root context's open state publicly. Run:

```
rg 'export.*use(Select|Dialog|AlertDialog|Popover|Menu|HoverCard|PreviewCard|Tooltip)RootContext' node_modules/@base-ui/react
```

Classify each of the 8 callers as:
- (a) root context is public → import and read `.open` from it
- (b) open state reachable via a prop on Root → drill through
- (c) neither → use a local `useState` driven by `MutationObserver` on the popup element's `data-state` attribute

Write classifications as an executor deviation before coding.

**Then**:
- Add `open: boolean` as a **required** field on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`.
- In `syncRegistration`, early-return when `!open` (popping any existing registration first).
- Thread the explicit open state through all 8 callers: `dialog.tsx`, `alert-dialog.tsx`, `lightbox.tsx`, `select.tsx`, `popover.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx` (two call sites: menu + submenu), `hover-card.tsx`, `tooltip.tsx`.
- Delete `isOverlayActuallyOpen` from `src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`. Revert `getTopModalOverlay` to check only `overlay.modal`.

### WP4 — Settings bootstrap collapse (DESIGN SPIKE required)

`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts` has two useEffect blocks that copy React Query data into a zustand store via `bootstrapEntity` (one for custom-mode ~L275-302, one for RQ mode ~L304-359). Baseline adds a `lastBootstrapRef` self-tracking guard to stop an infinite loop. The ref is a band-aid.

**Design spike before any code change**: answer in a written executor deviation.

- **SPIKE-Q1**: What external consumers read `createEntityStore.entities[id].cleanSnapshot`? Run `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src` and classify each hit as internal-to-createEntityStore / external consumer / test.
- **SPIKE-Q2**: How does a multi-tab save propagate into the local store's cleanSnapshot today? Trace: another tab writes → Supabase realtime → React Query cache invalidation → `useToolSettings` refetches → `authoritativeSettings` identity changes → bootstrap effect re-fires → store cleanSnapshot updates. Confirm each step in the code.
- **SPIKE-Q3**: Which React Query signal replaces `authoritativeSettings` in the effect's deps without identity flutter? `dataUpdatedAt: number` from `useQuery` is the candidate — it's monotonic and changes only on real data updates. Verify `useToolSettings` exposes it or can surface it.

If SPIKE-Q1 reveals a load-bearing external consumer of `cleanSnapshot` not tolerable to break, **STOP and emit a blocked deviation**. Do not proceed.

**Then**:
- Collapse the two bootstrap effects into a single effect keyed on `[activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, dataUpdatedAt]`.
- Store `authoritativeSettings` in a ref (`authoritativeSettingsRef`) updated every render.
- The single effect reads seed from the ref, not from a dep.
- Preserve the `customLoadSave` / `bootstrapData` path semantics (gate via `isCustomMode`).
- Delete `lastBootstrapRef` and all references to it.

### WP5 — Port WP4 pattern to useLastUsedEditSettings

`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts` L155-179 has the same RQ→store sync anti-pattern. After WP4 lands, port the same shape here.

- One effect keyed on stable signals.
- RQ seed (dbSettings) through a ref.
- Delete the store-state-based deep-equal guard.

### WP6 — Timeline render-body bootstrap narrow fix

`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts:814` calls `syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap` in the render body (not a useEffect). This is genuinely compensating for a first-paint ordering issue: subscribing descendants like `AgentChat` would read placeholder values if the sync moved to `useLayoutEffect`. A full redesign is OUT OF SCOPE.

Narrow fix:
- Add `const didBootstrapRef = useRef(false)` near the top of `useTimelineState`.
- Guard the sync call: if `didBootstrapRef.current` return early; else call the sync and set `didBootstrapRef.current = true`.
- Add a code comment explaining the first-paint constraint and that a deeper redesign is deferred.
- Do NOT move the sync to `useLayoutEffect`.
- Do NOT redesign `createTimelineStore`.

### WP7 — State ownership doc + CLAUDE.md link

- Create `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md`, under 80 lines, containing:
  - One-paragraph Purpose.
  - Source-of-truth table: RQ owns server-backed data; zustand owns ephemeral UI state; Context owns provider-scoped handles. They are not mirrors.
  - Three explicit "do nots" with bad/good example pairs:
    1. Do not useEffect-copy React Query data into a zustand store.
    2. Do not register overlays on ref attachment; gate on explicit `open` prop.
    3. Do not keep a context hook alongside a zustand store that hold the same state.
  - Short paragraph pointing readers to `git log megaplan/m7-state-cleanup` for worked examples.
- Add a short link to `CLAUDE.md` (1-3 lines, under a State Management heading or near Context hooks) pointing at the new doc.

## Success criteria (all must pass before M7 is done)

### Static

1. `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` → empty
2. `rg '\[PanesBoot\]|\[OverlayStack\]|\[AutoSave\]' src --glob '!*.test.*'` → empty
3. `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` → empty
4. `rg 'lastBootstrapRef' src` → empty
5. `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src` returns only internal `createEntityStore` uses and tests
6. `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` exists, `wc -l` under 80
7. `rg 'state_ownership' CLAUDE.md` returns at least one match

### Build / test

8. `npx tsc --noEmit` passes clean
9. `npm test` passes (all vitest suites)
10. New tests exist and pass:
    - `src/shared/state/__tests__/panesStore.bootstrap.test.tsx` — rerender-twice, zero setState-in-render warnings
    - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/__tests__/select.closed.test.tsx` — closed Select does not lock body.pointerEvents
    - `src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` — 5x deep-equal rerender fires bootstrap once; genuine refetch fires again
    - `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` — same shape as above
    - unit test covering `useTimelineState` didBootstrapRef guard

### Runtime (deferred_human — mark as skipped at execute, human verifies after)

11. Travel-between-images route loads without "Maximum update depth exceeded"
12. App is clickable on page load (body.pointerEvents stays default)
13. Video-editor route loads, AgentChat and timeline descendants see real values on first render, no setState-in-render warnings

## Out of scope

- Do not rewrite `createEntityStore` itself.
- Do not change `useToolSettings` public API.
- Do not redesign timeline store initialization.
- Do not add lint rules or enforcement tooling (the doc is the rule).
- Do not modify non-modal overlay behavior beyond threading `open`.
- Do not modify Base UI version or add dependencies.
- Do not reformat files beyond the changed lines.

## Executor hard rules

1. **Browser/runtime tasks**: return `status=skipped` on the FIRST encounter — never `status=blocked`. Blocked retries waste iteration budget and have stalled past chains. The runtime checks under "deferred_human" above are skipped explicitly.

2. **Evidence for done tasks**: every task with `status=done` MUST include at least one non-empty entry in `commands_run`, even if `files_changed` is empty. For tasks where prior work remains correct, include a verification command as evidence (e.g., `rg -l pattern src`, `git diff --stat path`, `npx tsc --noEmit`). Empty `commands_run` + empty `files_changed` on a done task triggers audit block.

3. **No RQ → zustand sync via useEffect**: this is the M1 tiebreaker. NEVER introduce an effect that depends on a React Query result AND calls a zustand store setter. If the scope seems to require it, stop and surface as a deviation for critic review.

4. **Overlay registration is gated on explicit open prop** — WP3 establishes this as a repo invariant.

5. **Architecture constraint**: the M1-M6 chain added stores that mirror state from other systems. This milestone REDUCES mirrors, never adds them. Every code change should delete more than it adds, or keep the ratio neutral. If a work package produces net-positive LOC, surface the reason as a deviation.

6. **Design spike precedes implementation** for WP4. No code for WP4 until spike questions are answered in an executor deviation.

7. **Narrow scope** for WP6. If the plan balloons into a timeline-store redesign, stop and surface as a blocked deviation.

# Implementation Plan: M7 — State-management Refactor Cleanup

## Overview

The M1-M6 chain shipped state-management code with three browser-breaking regressions, all traceable to zustand stores mirroring state already owned elsewhere (React Query, Context, Base UI popup open-state) with feedback-looping sync bridges. Baseline commit `5fa6917c9` contains band-aid patches + diagnostic logs. This milestone replaces the band-aids with proper fixes and removes the mirror pattern at 7 sites. All 7 work packages are bundled.

Current branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup`. Worktree: `/private/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`.

**Confirmed touch points from audit:**
- WP1: `usePaneLockPolicyState` lives at `src/shared/contexts/usePaneLockPolicyState.ts` (lines 21-128). Single external reader: `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:480` (inside `useBootstrapPanesStore`). Also has a dedicated test file (`usePaneLockPolicyState.test.ts`) and a mock in `PanesContext.test.tsx`.
- WP2: 12 isolated `console.log/warn` calls tagged `[PanesBoot]` / `[OverlayStack]` / `[AutoSave]`. Locations: `overlayStack.ts:150,161,172,204,246,249` and `panesStore.ts:392,396,512,518,524,531`. No helper wrappers to remove.
- WP3: 8 callers of `useOverlayBridge` confirmed in `dialog.tsx`, `alert-dialog.tsx`, `select.tsx`, `popover.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx` (two sites), `hover-card.tsx`, `tooltip.tsx`, `lightbox.tsx`. `isOverlayActuallyOpen` at `overlayStack.ts:88-101`; `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112`. `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30`.
- WP4: 2 bootstrap effects at `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:280-307` (custom mode) and `:309-359` (RQ mode). `lastBootstrapRef` declared at `:148-150`. `useToolSettings` (`src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:296-303`) currently does NOT expose `dataUpdatedAt`.
- WP5: Mirror pattern at `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:155-179` with `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, nextSeed)` guard.
- WP6: `syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap` called at render-body in `useTimelineState.ts:814`; function defined at `:401-411`.
- WP7: `docs/structure_detail/` exists (26 files). CLAUDE.md has a "Context hooks" section at lines 41-44.

## Phase 1: Cleanup (WP1, WP2) — low-risk deletions

**Scope:** Small
1. **Inline** the body of `usePaneLockPolicyState` (from `src/shared/contexts/usePaneLockPolicyState.ts:21-128`) directly into `useBootstrapPanesStore` at `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:480`. Collapse `usePaneLocks / setLocks / isGenerationsPaneOpenState` etc. into local hooks inside `useBootstrapPanesStore`.

If the existing test only validated the hook's contract (which is now inlined), prune redundant assertions but keep coverage of the panes-store bootstrap flow.

**Scope:** Small

## Phase 2: Overlay Bridge Correction (WP3)

### Step 3: Pre-check Base UI root context exposure (executor deviation required)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Classify** each of 8 overlay primitives (Select, Dialog, AlertDialog, Popover, Menu (×2 main+sub), HoverCard/PreviewCard, Tooltip). For each, run:
   ```
   rg 'export.*use(Select|Dialog|AlertDialog|Popover|Menu|PreviewCard|Tooltip)RootContext' node_modules/@base-ui/react
   ```
   Also inspect `node_modules/@base-ui/react/{primitive}/index.{js,d.ts}` for the exported API surface (Root, Trigger, Popup, `open` prop, subscription hooks).
2. **Emit** executor deviation note with 8 rows classifying each as (a) root context public → import and read `.open`; (b) open reachable via prop on `<Root>` → thread through; or (c) neither → use `useState` + `MutationObserver` on `data-state`.
3. **Do** classification BEFORE implementing the code changes in Step 4.

### Step 4: Overlay bridge requires explicit `open` prop (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx` and 8 callers)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `open: boolean` as a required field on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30`.
2. **Modify** `syncRegistration` at `overlayBridge.tsx:136-161`: early-return when `!open`, popping any existing registration. The branch becomes:
   ```typescript
   if (!open || elements.length === 0) {
     if (registeredRef.current) {
       stackApi.getState().popOverlay(handle.id);
       registeredRef.current = false;
     }
     return;
   }
   ```
3. **Thread** `open` through `useCallback` deps and add it to useEffect watch deps where `syncRegistration` is attached.
4. **Update 8 callers** — for each, use the classification outcome from Step 3:
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dialog.tsx:68` — `DialogContent`. Base UI Dialog exposes `DialogRootContext` via internal; if public, use it; else consumer component must accept an `open` prop drilled from Dialog.Root.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/alert-dialog.tsx:56` — same shape.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/lightbox.tsx:38` — `LightboxDialogPortal`. Read from DialogRootContext (lightbox is a Dialog).
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/select.tsx:198` — `SelectContent`. Read from SelectRootContext.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/popover.tsx:50` — `PopoverContent`.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dropdown-menu.tsx:83` (submenu) and `:140` (main menu) — both read Menu context; submenu reads sub-menu context.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/hover-card.tsx:40` — `HoverCardContent`. Uses Base UI PreviewCard primitive.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/tooltip.tsx:49` — `TooltipContent`.
5. **For callers classified as (c)** (no public context/prop): inside the wrapper component, `useState` backed by a `MutationObserver` that watches `data-state` / `data-open` on the popup element. Attach observer in `useEffect`, disconnect on cleanup.
6. **Delete** `isOverlayActuallyOpen` from `src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts:88-101`. **Revert** `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112` to return based on `overlay.modal` alone (loop from end, return first `overlay.modal === true`).
7. **Update** existing overlay tests (`overlayBridge.test.tsx`, `overlayStack.test.ts`, `lightbox.test.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlaySequences.test.tsx`) for the new required `open` prop.

**Scope:** Small

2. **Assert** `overlayStack` has no modal entry registered for the closed select.

## Phase 3: Settings Bootstrap Collapse (WP4, WP5)

### Step 6: WP4 design spike (executor deviation required before coding)
**Scope:** Small
1. **SPIKE-Q1** — External consumers of `cleanSnapshot`: run `rg -n 'cleanSnapshot' src` and classify each hit:
   - Internal to `createEntityStore` (expected)
   - External consumer (needs careful handling)
   - Test file (update tests; don't gate behavior)
2. **SPIKE-Q2** — Multi-tab save propagation: trace `Supabase realtime → RQ cache invalidation → useToolSettings refetch → authoritativeSettings identity change → bootstrap effect → store.cleanSnapshot updates`. Read `useToolSettings.ts`, check for realtime subscriptions or RQ `invalidateQueries` calls. Confirm each step in code.
3. **SPIKE-Q3** — Stable RQ signal: `useToolSettings` currently does NOT expose `dataUpdatedAt` (confirmed in audit at `src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:296-303`). **Options:**
   - (a) Expand `useToolSettings` return to include `dataUpdatedAt` (additive, not a public-API break).
   - (b) Rely on RQ structural sharing: if `authoritativeSettings` reference is stable across refetches of identical data (RQ default behavior), use identity directly; guard via `useRef` snapshot comparison only when identity changes.
   - Preferred: (a) — it is explicit, monotonic, and self-documenting.
4. **Emit** executor deviation summarizing SPIKE-Q1/Q2/Q3 answers. If SPIKE-Q1 reveals a load-bearing external consumer of `cleanSnapshot` whose behavior changes with the new effect shape, STOP and emit a blocked deviation.

### Step 7: Collapse settings bootstrap (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** (if spike chose option a) `dataUpdatedAt` to `useToolSettings`'s return in `src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:296-303` by surfacing `query.dataUpdatedAt` from `useQuery`. Update consumers; expose in the hook's return type.
2. **Store** `authoritativeSettings` in a ref:
   ```typescript
   const authoritativeSettingsRef = useRef(authoritativeSettings);
   authoritativeSettingsRef.current = authoritativeSettings;
   ```
3. **Collapse** the two bootstrap effects at `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:280-307` and `:309-359` into a single effect keyed on `[activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, dataUpdatedAt, isCustomMode]`:
   ```typescript
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!isEntityValid || authoritativeIsLoading || authoritativeError) return;
     if (isCustomMode) {
       if (!bootstrapData) return;
       const seed = cloneValue(bootstrapData);
       bootstrapEntity({ entityId: activeEntityId, db: null, lastUsed: seed });
       return;
     }
     const seed = cloneValue(authoritativeSettingsRef.current ?? defaults);
     bootstrapEntity({
       entityId: activeEntityId,
       db: hasShotSettings ? seed : null,
       lastUsed: hasShotSettings ? null : seed,
     });
   }, [activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, dataUpdatedAt, isCustomMode, ...]);
   ```
4. **Preserve** `reloadStoredEntity` effect at `:273-278` (unrelated to the mirror pattern — it's just a reload trigger).
5. **Delete** `lastBootstrapRef` declaration at `:148-150` and all references.
6. **Delete** the `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, ...)` guards (lines 291, 335) — the effect's key-based gating replaces them.
7. **Preserve** `bootstrapData` vs `customLoadSave` semantics via the `isCustomMode` branch.

1. Run and verify empty / expected:
   - `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` → empty (WP1)
   - `rg '\[PanesBoot\]|\[OverlayStack\]|\[AutoSave\]' src --glob '!*.test.*'` → empty (WP2)
   - `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` → empty (WP3)
   - `rg 'lastBootstrapRef' src` → empty (WP4)
   - `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src` → only `createEntityStore.ts` internal + tests (WP4/5)
   - `wc -l docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` under 80 (WP7)
   - `rg 'state_ownership' CLAUDE.md` → ≥1 match (WP7)

### Step 17: Build + test verification
**Scope:** Small
1. `npx tsc --noEmit` — clean typecheck.
2. `npm test` — all vitest suites pass, including 5 new tests (panes bootstrap, select closed, useAutoSaveSettings bootstrap, useLastUsedEditSettings bootstrap, useTimelineState bootstrap guard).

### Step 18: Runtime smoke (deferred — skipped on first pass)
**Scope:** Small
1. Per executor hard rule #1, return `status=skipped` for the three runtime checks on first encounter:
   - Travel-between-images route loads without "Maximum update depth exceeded"
   - App clickable on page load (body.pointerEvents stays default)
   - Video-editor route loads, AgentChat and timeline descendants see real values on first render, no setState-in-render warnings
2. Human verifies post-merge per `deferred_human` convention.

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 (WP1, WP2) — clean baseline first; low risk.
2. Phase 2 (WP3) — pre-check spike → overlay bridge changes → closed-select test. Establishes the `open` invariant.
3. Phase 3 (WP4, WP5) — spike → collapse useAutoSaveSettings → test → port pattern to useLastUsedEditSettings → test. WP5 depends on WP4 pattern.
4. Phase 4 (WP6) — narrow ref guard + test. Independent.
5. Phase 5 (panes bootstrap test) — validates WP1 inlining.
6. Phase 6 (WP7) — doc only after code lands so examples are accurate.
7. Phase 7 — static + typecheck + test sweep last.

## Validation Order
1. After each WP, run the targeted rg / test for that package (cheap).
2. After Phase 3, run `npx tsc --noEmit` — settings types are interconnected.
3. After Phase 6, full `npm test` sweep + all static evidence checks.
4. Runtime checks deferred to human per executor hard rule #1.

## Architecture invariants enforced
- **No RQ→zustand useEffect sync.** WP4/WP5 effects key on stable signals (`dataUpdatedAt` / entity id) with refs holding seed data. No `useEffect(() => setStore(rqData), [rqData])`.
- **Overlay registration gated on `open`.** WP3 makes `open` required at the type level; registration occurs only when `open === true`.
- **Net code reduction.** Deleting `isOverlayActuallyOpen`, `lastBootstrapRef`, deepEqual guards, 12 console logs, `usePaneLockPolicyState.ts`, and its test file. New adds: single effect, `didBootstrapRef`, 5 test files, 1 doc. Expect net-negative LOC across production code (tests and docs are separate).

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "refactor-pinned-shot-groups-20260408-0508"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-175",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the real callers of the safe timeline hooks in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that code passes through both mounted-editor values and the `null` outside-provider case, then uses `ops.registergenerationasset`, `ops.handleassetdrop`, and `data.resolvedconfig?.clips` when available, so any replacement api has to preserve both nullable access and the current immediate-drop contract rather than only exposing non-null store selectors.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-176",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` as a caller of `usetimelineeditordata()`. it only reads `dataref`, but it passes that mutable ref straight into `useclipresizegesture`, which relies on latest timeline data during pointer sequences; the plan does not yet say how this exact caller will receive a stable mutable adapter once the context is removed.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-177",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/videoeditorshell.tsx`, where `handlekeyboarddelete` combines `editordata.dataref.current` with `editordata.selectedclipids` to build a mutation before falling back to `handledeleteclips`. that caller needs synchronized latest-ref and latest-selection semantics, so splitting those reads across unrelated store selectors without an adapter can change behavior even if each field is individually correct.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    }

# M7 — State-management refactor cleanup (7 work packages bundled)

## Context

The M1-M6 megaplan chain shipped state-management code that caused three browser-breaking regressions, all from the same anti-pattern: zustand stores mirror state already owned elsewhere (React Query, React Context, Base UI popup open-state), with sync bridges that feedback-loop.

Baseline commit 5fa6917c9 (plus a small follow-up lightbox keyboard-nav fix at 8c613d041) contains band-aid patches that unblock the app with diagnostic console logs. This milestone replaces the band-aids with proper fixes and removes the underlying mirror pattern at 7 sites. All 7 work packages are bundled here because several are trivial and several are interdependent.

Current branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup`, worktree at `/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`.

## Work packages (execute in order; each is a distinct unit with its own success criteria)

### WP1 — Delete panes context zombie

Grep audit showed `usePaneLockPolicyState` has no readers outside `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts` itself. The context is dead weight the M1-M6 refactor forgot to remove.

- Find the `usePaneLockPolicyState` definition: `rg -l 'function usePaneLockPolicyState\|export.*usePaneLockPolicyState' src`.
- Inline its body directly into `useBootstrapPanesStore` in `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`.
- Delete the source file, any tests that only covered it, and any mocks that imported from it.
- After: `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` returns nothing.

### WP2 — Strip diagnostic logs

Baseline added console.log/warn tagged `[PanesBoot]`, `[OverlayStack]`, `[AutoSave]`. Diagnosis is done.

- Enumerate: `rg '\[PanesBoot\]|\[OverlayStack\]|\[AutoSave\]' src --glob '!*.test.*'`.
- Delete every matched console.log/console.warn statement and any nearby object-literal helpers that exist only to build the log payload.
- Do NOT touch other console statements. Do NOT touch test files.
- After: the rg above returns nothing.

### WP3 — Overlay bridge requires explicit open prop (pre-check required)

The Select wrapper registers its popup with the overlay stack at ref attachment, regardless of whether the Select is open. Baseline patches this by gating `getTopModalOverlay` on DOM `data-state`/`data-open` attributes via `isOverlayActuallyOpen` in `src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`. This is a fragile workaround.

**Pre-check before coding**: verify Base UI exposes the root context's open state publicly. Run:

```
rg 'export.*use(Select|Dialog|AlertDialog|Popover|Menu|HoverCard|PreviewCard|Tooltip)RootContext' node_modules/@base-ui/react
```

Classify each of the 8 callers as:
- (a) root context is public → import and read `.open` from it
- (b) open state reachable via a prop on Root → drill through
- (c) neither → use a local `useState` driven by `MutationObserver` on the popup element's `data-state` attribute

Write classifications as an executor deviation before coding.

**Then**:
- Add `open: boolean` as a **required** field on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`.
- In `syncRegistration`, early-return when `!open` (popping any existing registration first).
- Thread the explicit open state through all 8 callers: `dialog.tsx`, `alert-dialog.tsx`, `lightbox.tsx`, `select.tsx`, `popover.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx` (two call sites: menu + submenu), `hover-card.tsx`, `tooltip.tsx`.
- Delete `isOverlayActuallyOpen` from `src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`. Revert `getTopModalOverlay` to check only `overlay.modal`.

### WP4 — Settings bootstrap collapse (DESIGN SPIKE required)

`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts` has two useEffect blocks that copy React Query data into a zustand store via `bootstrapEntity` (one for custom-mode ~L275-302, one for RQ mode ~L304-359). Baseline adds a `lastBootstrapRef` self-tracking guard to stop an infinite loop. The ref is a band-aid.

**Design spike before any code change**: answer in a written executor deviation.

- **SPIKE-Q1**: What external consumers read `createEntityStore.entities[id].cleanSnapshot`? Run `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src` and classify each hit as internal-to-createEntityStore / external consumer / test.
- **SPIKE-Q2**: How does a multi-tab save propagate into the local store's cleanSnapshot today? Trace: another tab writes → Supabase realtime → React Query cache invalidation → `useToolSettings` refetches → `authoritativeSettings` identity changes → bootstrap effect re-fires → store cleanSnapshot updates. Confirm each step in the code.
- **SPIKE-Q3**: Which React Query signal replaces `authoritativeSettings` in the effect's deps without identity flutter? `dataUpdatedAt: number` from `useQuery` is the candidate — it's monotonic and changes only on real data updates. Verify `useToolSettings` exposes it or can surface it.

If SPIKE-Q1 reveals a load-bearing external consumer of `cleanSnapshot` not tolerable to break, **STOP and emit a blocked deviation**. Do not proceed.

**Then**:
- Collapse the two bootstrap effects into a single effect keyed on `[activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, dataUpdatedAt]`.
- Store `authoritativeSettings` in a ref (`authoritativeSettingsRef`) updated every render.
- The single effect reads seed from the ref, not from a dep.
- Preserve the `customLoadSave` / `bootstrapData` path semantics (gate via `isCustomMode`).
- Delete `lastBootstrapRef` and all references to it.

### WP5 — Port WP4 pattern to useLastUsedEditSettings

`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts` L155-179 has the same RQ→store sync anti-pattern. After WP4 lands, port the same shape here.

- One effect keyed on stable signals.
- RQ seed (dbSettings) through a ref.
- Delete the store-state-based deep-equal guard.

### WP6 — Timeline render-body bootstrap narrow fix

`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts:814` calls `syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap` in the render body (not a useEffect). This is genuinely compensating for a first-paint ordering issue: subscribing descendants like `AgentChat` would read placeholder values if the sync moved to `useLayoutEffect`. A full redesign is OUT OF SCOPE.

Narrow fix:
- Add `const didBootstrapRef = useRef(false)` near the top of `useTimelineState`.
- Guard the sync call: if `didBootstrapRef.current` return early; else call the sync and set `didBootstrapRef.current = true`.
- Add a code comment explaining the first-paint constraint and that a deeper redesign is deferred.
- Do NOT move the sync to `useLayoutEffect`.
- Do NOT redesign `createTimelineStore`.

### WP7 — State ownership doc + CLAUDE.md link

- Create `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md`, under 80 lines, containing:
  - One-paragraph Purpose.
  - Source-of-truth table: RQ owns server-backed data; zustand owns ephemeral UI state; Context owns provider-scoped handles. They are not mirrors.
  - Three explicit "do nots" with bad/good example pairs:
    1. Do not useEffect-copy React Query data into a zustand store.
    2. Do not register overlays on ref attachment; gate on explicit `open` prop.
    3. Do not keep a context hook alongside a zustand store that hold the same state.
  - Short paragraph pointing readers to `git log megaplan/m7-state-cleanup` for worked examples.
- Add a short link to `CLAUDE.md` (1-3 lines, under a State Management heading or near Context hooks) pointing at the new doc.

## Success criteria (all must pass before M7 is done)

### Static

1. `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` → empty
2. `rg '\[PanesBoot\]|\[OverlayStack\]|\[AutoSave\]' src --glob '!*.test.*'` → empty
3. `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` → empty
4. `rg 'lastBootstrapRef' src` → empty
5. `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src` returns only internal `createEntityStore` uses and tests
6. `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` exists, `wc -l` under 80
7. `rg 'state_ownership' CLAUDE.md` returns at least one match

### Build / test

8. `npx tsc --noEmit` passes clean
9. `npm test` passes (all vitest suites)
10. New tests exist and pass:
    - `src/shared/state/__tests__/panesStore.bootstrap.test.tsx` — rerender-twice, zero setState-in-render warnings
    - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/__tests__/select.closed.test.tsx` — closed Select does not lock body.pointerEvents
    - `src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` — 5x deep-equal rerender fires bootstrap once; genuine refetch fires again
    - `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` — same shape as above
    - unit test covering `useTimelineState` didBootstrapRef guard

### Runtime (deferred_human — mark as skipped at execute, human verifies after)

11. Travel-between-images route loads without "Maximum update depth exceeded"
12. App is clickable on page load (body.pointerEvents stays default)
13. Video-editor route loads, AgentChat and timeline descendants see real values on first render, no setState-in-render warnings

## Out of scope

- Do not rewrite `createEntityStore` itself.
- Do not change `useToolSettings` public API.
- Do not redesign timeline store initialization.
- Do not add lint rules or enforcement tooling (the doc is the rule).
- Do not modify non-modal overlay behavior beyond threading `open`.
- Do not modify Base UI version or add dependencies.
- Do not reformat files beyond the changed lines.

## Executor hard rules

1. **Browser/runtime tasks**: return `status=skipped` on the FIRST encounter — never `status=blocked`. Blocked retries waste iteration budget and have stalled past chains. The runtime checks under "deferred_human" above are skipped explicitly.

2. **Evidence for done tasks**: every task with `status=done` MUST include at least one non-empty entry in `commands_run`, even if `files_changed` is empty. For tasks where prior work remains correct, include a verification command as evidence (e.g., `rg -l pattern src`, `git diff --stat path`, `npx tsc --noEmit`). Empty `commands_run` + empty `files_changed` on a done task triggers audit block.

3. **No RQ → zustand sync via useEffect**: this is the M1 tiebreaker. NEVER introduce an effect that depends on a React Query result AND calls a zustand store setter. If the scope seems to require it, stop and surface as a deviation for critic review.

4. **Overlay registration is gated on explicit open prop** — WP3 establishes this as a repo invariant.

5. **Architecture constraint**: the M1-M6 chain added stores that mirror state from other systems. This milestone REDUCES mirrors, never adds them. Every code change should delete more than it adds, or keep the ratio neutral. If a work package produces net-positive LOC, surface the reason as a deviation.

6. **Design spike precedes implementation** for WP4. No code for WP4 until spike questions are answered in an executor deviation.

7. **Narrow scope** for WP6. If the plan balloons into a timeline-store redesign, stop and surface as a blocked deviation.

# Implementation Plan: M7 — State-management Refactor Cleanup (revised)

## Overview

The M1-M6 chain shipped state-management code with browser-breaking regressions from zustand stores mirroring state owned elsewhere (RQ, Context, Base UI open state) with feedback-looping sync bridges. Baseline `5fa6917c9` contains band-aid patches + diagnostic logs. M7 replaces band-aids with proper fixes and removes the mirror pattern.

Current branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup`. Worktree: `/private/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`.

**Revisions in this pass** (addressing critic flags):

1. **WP4 spike premise corrected** (FLAG-M7-001, issue_hints-2): Repo has no settings realtime path — `useSettingsInvalidation.ts` is a stub, `useToolSettings` uses `staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000`, and window-focus refetch is disabled (`queryDefaults.ts:13-17`). Therefore `authoritativeSettings` identity flutter from refetches is not a realistic concern today. The effect can key on stable identifiers alone; `dataUpdatedAt` is unneeded.
2. **`useToolSettings` public API untouched** (FLAG-M7-002, issue_hints-1): Remove the proposed `dataUpdatedAt` expansion. Keep the hook's return contract at `src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:145-161, 296-303` exactly as it is today.
3. **WP3 re-architected** (FLAG-M7-003, scope, all_locations-1/2, callers): Content wrappers **self-source `open`** from Base UI root context (or MutationObserver on `data-state` when context is not public). Callers of `<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, `<Tooltip>`, etc. are **not modified** — both controlled and uncontrolled call shapes continue to work. Raw-Root aliases in `tooltip.tsx:15` and `hover-card.tsx:8` are unchanged.
4. **WP6 narrowed** (FLAG-M7-004, correctness-1): Drop `didBootstrapRef` — the store's existing `availability.mounted` guard at `useTimelineState.ts:401-411` is the correct check. WP6 collapses to: verify the existing guard via a test and add a code comment documenting the render-body pattern. No code change to the bootstrap call itself.
5. **Base UI pre-check is executable** (correctness-2): `node_modules/@base-ui/react` absent → prerequisite `npm install` step added; classification inspects `.d.ts` surface when the package is present, else fall through to MutationObserver for all.

**Confirmed touch points from audit (unchanged):**
- WP1: `usePaneLockPolicyState` at `src/shared/contexts/usePaneLockPolicyState.ts:21-128`. Single reader at `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:480`. Dedicated test + `PanesContext.test.tsx` mock.
- WP2: 12 isolated `console.log/warn` calls in `overlayStack.ts:150,161,172,204,246,249` and `panesStore.ts:392,396,512,518,524,531`.
- WP3: 8 content wrappers (`dialog.tsx`, `alert-dialog.tsx`, `select.tsx`, `popover.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx` ×2, `hover-card.tsx`, `tooltip.tsx`, `lightbox.tsx`). `isOverlayActuallyOpen` at `overlayStack.ts:88-101`; `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112`. `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30`.
- WP4: bootstrap effects at `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:280-307, 309-359`; `lastBootstrapRef` at `:148-150`.
- WP5: `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:155-179`.
- WP6: `syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap` call at `useTimelineState.ts:814`; definition at `:401-411` (already short-circuits on `availability.mounted`). Store at `timelineStore.ts:362-387` flips the flag on first `syncSlices`.
- WP7: `docs/structure_detail/` exists; CLAUDE.md "Context hooks" at `:41-44`.

## Phase 1: Cleanup (WP1, WP2) — low-risk deletions

### Step 1: Delete panes context zombie (`src/shared/contexts/usePaneLockPolicyState.ts`, `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inline** the body of `usePaneLockPolicyState` (lines 21-128) directly into `useBootstrapPanesStore` at `panesStore.ts:480`. Collapse local hook state (`useUserUIState('paneLocks', UNLOCKED_PANES)`, `useIsMobile`, `useIsTablet`, `useState` for open-states, effect for mobile lock sync, setter factories) inline.
2. **Move or preserve** `UNLOCKED_PANES` constant — if defined in `usePaneLockPolicyState.ts`, move its definition to `panesStore.ts` (or a constants module it already shares with).
3. **Delete** `src/shared/contexts/usePaneLockPolicyState.ts`.
4. **Delete** `src/shared/contexts/usePaneLockPolicyState.test.ts` (dedicated file).
5. **Rewrite** `src/shared/contexts/__tests__/PanesContext.test.tsx` to mock `useUserUIState` / `useIsMobile` / `useIsTablet` directly (remove `vi.mock('...usePaneLockPolicyState...')` at lines 6,18,113,150). Preserve bootstrap coverage; prune tests that only validated the now-inlined hook contract.
6. **Verify** `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` returns empty.

### Step 2: Strip diagnostic logs (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`, `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Delete** the 6 `console.log/warn` calls in `overlayStack.ts` at lines 150, 161, 172, 204, 246, 249 (surrounding `if` blocks remain). Delete the helper object literals **only if** they are defined inline inside the `console.*` argument expressions (audit confirmed they are — no helper functions to remove).
2. **Delete** the 6 `console.log` calls in `panesStore.ts` at lines 392, 396, 512, 518, 524, 531.
3. **Do not** touch any other `console.*` or test files.
4. **Verify** `rg '\[PanesBoot\]|\[OverlayStack\]|\[AutoSave\]' src --glob '!*.test.*'` returns empty.

## Phase 2: Overlay Bridge Correction (WP3) — re-architected

### Step 3: Ensure Base UI is installed, pre-check root context exposure (executor deviation required)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Install** if missing: check `ls node_modules/@base-ui/react 2>/dev/null`; if absent, run `npm install` (declared in `package.json:46`). If install still fails, emit a blocked deviation.
2. **Classify** each of 8 primitives (Select, Dialog, AlertDialog, Popover, Menu (×2), HoverCard/PreviewCard, Tooltip) by inspecting the `.d.ts` surface of `node_modules/@base-ui/react/{primitive}/index.d.ts`:
   ```
   rg -l 'export.*use(Select|Dialog|AlertDialog|Popover|Menu|PreviewCard|Tooltip)RootContext' node_modules/@base-ui/react
   ```
   Also grep for any publicly exported hook that returns `{ open }` or a subscription to open state.
3. **Emit** executor deviation with 8 rows classifying each primitive as:
   - (a) public root-context hook → import and read `.open` from it inside the Content wrapper
   - (c) no public hook → use local `useState` inside the Content wrapper, driven by a `MutationObserver` on the popup element that watches `data-state` (and/or `data-open` attribute) transitions. Attach the observer when `bridge.registerElement('popup', node)` receives the node; disconnect on unmount.
4. **Note** that callers of `<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, `<Tooltip>`, etc. are **not** modified — the Content wrapper sources `open` internally. This preserves uncontrolled call shapes (e.g., `TrackLabel.tsx:130-143`, `TaskLogFilters.tsx:51-62`, `FinalVideoSectionControls.tsx:100-115`, `dialog.test.tsx:31-39`, `select.test.tsx:15-20`).

### Step 4: Make `open` a required bridge option and wire 8 content wrappers (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx` + 8 files)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `open: boolean` as a required field on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30`.
2. **Modify** `syncRegistration` at `overlayBridge.tsx:136-161`: early-return when `!open`, popping any existing registration first:
   ```typescript
   if (!open || elements.length === 0) {
     if (registeredRef.current) {
       stackApi.getState().popOverlay(handle.id);
       registeredRef.current = false;
     }
     return;
   }
   ```
3. **Thread** `open` through `useCallback` deps and ensure `syncRegistration` runs whenever `open` toggles.
4. **Update 8 Content wrappers** — each wrapper sources `open` from the Step 3 classification:
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dialog.tsx:68` — `DialogContent` reads open via the classified source for Dialog.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/alert-dialog.tsx:56` — `AlertDialogContent` reads via AlertDialog classification.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/lightbox.tsx:38` — `LightboxDialogPortal` reads via Dialog classification (same primitive).
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/select.tsx:198` — `SelectContent` reads via Select classification.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/popover.tsx:50` — `PopoverContent` reads via Popover classification.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dropdown-menu.tsx:140` (main) and `:83` (submenu) — `DropdownMenuContent` and `DropdownMenuSubContent` read via Menu classification.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/hover-card.tsx:40` — `HoverCardContent` reads via PreviewCard classification.
   - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/tooltip.tsx:49` — `TooltipContent` reads via Tooltip classification.
5. **For classification (c)** wrappers, add the MutationObserver helper locally. Example shape:
   ```tsx
   const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
   React.useEffect(() => {
     const node = popupElementRef.current;
     if (!node) return;
     const read = () => {
       const state = node.getAttribute('data-state');
       setOpen(state === 'open' || node.hasAttribute('data-open'));
     };
     read();
     const mo = new MutationObserver(read);
     mo.observe(node, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-state', 'data-open'] });
     return () => mo.disconnect();
   }, []);
   ```
6. **Delete** `isOverlayActuallyOpen` from `overlayStack.ts:88-101`. **Revert** `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112` to iterate from end and return the first `overlay.modal === true`.
7. **Do not change** callers of `<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, `<Tooltip>`, `<HoverCard>`, `<Select>`, `<DropdownMenu>`, `<AlertDialog>`, `<Lightbox>`. Raw-Root aliases at `tooltip.tsx:15` and `hover-card.tsx:8` stay as-is.
8. **Update** existing overlay tests (`overlayBridge.test.tsx`, `overlayStack.test.ts`, `lightbox.test.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlaySequences.test.tsx`, plus any Content-level unit tests): tests that directly call `useOverlayBridge` must now pass `open`. Tests rendering `<Content>` wrappers don't need changes since wrappers self-source.

**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** test rendering `<Select defaultValue="">` with `<SelectContent>` children, without opening. Assert `document.body.style.pointerEvents` stays default (not `'none'`).
2. **Assert** overlay stack has zero modal entries for the unopened select.

## Phase 3: Settings Bootstrap Collapse (WP4, WP5) — simplified

### Step 6: WP4 design spike (executor deviation required before coding)
**Scope:** Small
1. **SPIKE-Q1** — `rg -n 'cleanSnapshot' src`. Classify each hit as: internal to `createEntityStore` / external consumer / test. If any external consumer's behavior depends on the effect's firing order, STOP and emit a blocked deviation.
2. **SPIKE-Q2** — Multi-tab propagation: confirm the negative finding in code:
   - `src/shared/hooks/invalidation/useSettingsInvalidation.ts` is a type-only stub (no active invalidation).
   - `src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:200-213` sets `staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000`.
   - `src/shared/lib/query/queryDefaults.ts:13-17` disables window-focus refetch.
   - Conclusion: under current repo state, settings do not re-propagate from other tabs. `authoritativeSettings` identity is stable after initial load (RQ structural sharing preserves object identity across rerenders with equal data). Therefore no `dataUpdatedAt`-style tiebreaker is needed.
3. **SPIKE-Q3** — Skipped. The premise that `authoritativeSettings` identity flutters does not hold in this codebase. Do not expand `useToolSettings` public API.
4. **Emit** executor deviation summarizing SPIKE-Q1 + negative SPIKE-Q2 + SPIKE-Q3 dropped.

### Step 7: Collapse settings bootstrap (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Do not modify** `src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts`. Its public return contract stays as-is (`:145-161, 296-303`).
2. **Store** `authoritativeSettings` and `bootstrapData` in refs updated every render:
   ```typescript
   const authoritativeSettingsRef = useRef(authoritativeSettings);
   authoritativeSettingsRef.current = authoritativeSettings;
   const bootstrapDataRef = useRef(bootstrapData);
   bootstrapDataRef.current = bootstrapData;
   ```
3. **Collapse** the two bootstrap effects at `:280-307` and `:309-359` into a single effect keyed on `[activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, isCustomMode, isEntityValid, authoritativeIsLoading, authoritativeError]`:
   ```typescript
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!isEntityValid || authoritativeIsLoading || authoritativeError) return;
     if (isCustomMode) {
       const seed = bootstrapDataRef.current;
       if (!seed) return;
       bootstrapEntity({ entityId: activeEntityId, db: null, lastUsed: cloneValue(seed) });
       return;
     }
     const seed = cloneValue(authoritativeSettingsRef.current ?? defaults);
     bootstrapEntity({
       entityId: activeEntityId,
       db: hasShotSettings ? seed : null,
       lastUsed: hasShotSettings ? null : seed,
     });
   }, [activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, isCustomMode, isEntityValid, authoritativeIsLoading, authoritativeError, bootstrapEntity, defaults]);
   ```
4. **Preserve** the `reloadStoredEntity` effect at `:273-278` unchanged (not a mirror).
5. **Delete** `lastBootstrapRef` at `:148-150` and all references.
6. **Delete** the `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, ...)` guards at `:291, :335` — the effect's key-based gating replaces them. `bootstrapEntity` is idempotent for identical payloads per the store's internal guards.
7. **Preserve** `customLoadSave` vs `bootstrapData` semantics via the `isCustomMode` branch.

### Step 8: useAutoSaveSettings bootstrap test (`src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** test: render hook, rerender 5× with deep-equal (identity-stable — simulate RQ structural sharing) data, assert `bootstrapEntity` called exactly once. Then change `activeEntityId`, assert it fires a second time.
2. **Note** in a test comment: since settings RQ queries have 10-min staleTime and no realtime invalidation, the effect genuinely reruns only when `activeEntityId` or other keyed inputs change. If future work adds settings realtime, this test must be updated.

### Step 9: Port to `useLastUsedEditSettings` (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Store** `dbSettings` in a ref updated every render.
2. **Collapse** the effect at `:155-179` keyed on `[entityId, dbSettings !== null]` (boolean for `hasPersistedData`), reading seed from the ref:
   ```typescript
   const dbSettingsRef = useRef(dbSettings);
   dbSettingsRef.current = dbSettings;
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!entityId) return;
     const localSettings = readLocalStorageSettings(entityId);
     const dbSnapshot = dbSettingsRef.current ? { ...localSettings, ...dbSettingsRef.current } : null;
     bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db: dbSnapshot, lastUsed: localSettings });
   }, [entityId, dbSettings !== null, bootstrapEntity]);
   ```
3. **Delete** the `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, nextSeed)` guard and the `hasPersistedData === (dbSnapshot !== null)` check.

### Step 10: useLastUsedEditSettings bootstrap test (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** test mirroring Step 8: 5× rerender with identity-stable `dbSettings` fires bootstrap once; a null→non-null `dbSettings` transition (persistence-flag change) fires again.

## Phase 4: Timeline Verification (WP6) — narrowed

### Step 11: Verify existing guard + add explanatory comment (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Do not** add `didBootstrapRef`. The store-level guard at `syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap` (`useTimelineState.ts:401-411`) already short-circuits on `availability.mounted`, which is flipped true by `syncSlices` at `timelineStore.ts:362-387`. Per-component-instance guarding would be redundant because `storeRef.current` is per-hook-call.
2. **Add** a short code comment above the render-body call at `:814`:
   ```typescript
   // Render-body sync (not useLayoutEffect) so descendants like AgentChat see real
   // slice values on first paint. Idempotent via availability.mounted short-circuit
   // in syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap; see timelineStore.ts:362-387.
   ```
3. **Do NOT** move to `useLayoutEffect` or redesign the store.

**Scope:** Small

2. **Assert** no "Cannot update a component while rendering another component" warning is emitted across 3+ rerenders.

### Step 18: Runtime smoke (deferred — skipped per executor hard rule #1)
**Scope:** Small

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 (WP1, WP2) — clean baseline first.
2. Phase 2 (WP3) — Base UI install + pre-check → content-wrapper self-sourcing + overlayStack revert. Callers untouched.
3. Phase 3 (WP4, WP5) — spike confirms no realtime path → collapse effect with entity-id keying → ports to useLastUsedEditSettings.
4. Phase 4 (WP6) — verification test + code comment only. No production-code behavior change.
5. Phase 5 (panes test) — supports WP1.
6. Phase 6 (WP7) — doc + CLAUDE.md link.
7. Phase 7 — static evidence + typecheck + test sweep.

## Validation Order
1. Per-WP: run the targeted rg / test (cheap).
2. After Phase 3: `npx tsc --noEmit` (settings types interconnected).
3. After Phase 6: full `npm test` + all static checks.
4. Runtime: deferred human.

## Architecture invariants enforced
- **No RQ→zustand useEffect sync.** WP4/WP5 effects key on stable identifiers (entity id, boolean flags) with refs holding seed data. No effect depends on an RQ result object in its deps array.
- **Overlay registration gated on `open`.** `open` is required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions`. Content wrappers source it from Base UI root context or MutationObserver. Callers (`<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, etc.) unchanged.
- **`useToolSettings` public API untouched.** No `dataUpdatedAt` or other additive changes.
- **Timeline bootstrap behavior unchanged.** Existing `availability.mounted` guard trusted; test confirms idempotency.
- **Net code reduction.** Delete `isOverlayActuallyOpen`, `lastBootstrapRef`, deepEqual guards, 12 console logs, `usePaneLockPolicyState.ts`, its test. Add: single collapsed effect, MutationObserver helpers (only for (c)-classified primitives), 5 test files, 1 doc. Expect net-negative production LOC.

    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "issue_hints-2",
    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: SPIKE-Q2's expected multi-tab propagation path is not present in this checkout. `src/shared/hooks/invalidation/useSettingsInvalidation.ts` is only a stub, `src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:200-213` uses a static query with `staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000`, and `src/shared/lib/query/queryDefaults.ts:13-17` disables window-focus refetch, so the plan is relying on realtime/invalidation behavior that I could not confirm in code.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-1",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: WP6's proposed `didBootstrapRef` guard does not clearly change the behavior that already exists. `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts:401-411` already short-circuits `syncInitialTimelineStoreBootstrap()` once the store reports `availability.mounted`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/timelineStore.ts:362-387` marks the store mounted on the first `syncSlices()` call, so adding another ref-level guard looks redundant and may not affect the remaining render-time store write at all.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-2",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: WP3's required Base UI pre-check is not executable as written in this repository snapshot. `package.json:46` declares `@base-ui/react`, but there is no `node_modules/@base-ui/react` directory in the worktree, so the mandated `rg 'export.*use...RootContext' node_modules/@base-ui/react` command fails before any classification can happen.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: The overlay-open problem is broader than the 8 content call sites named in the plan because the root wrappers are inconsistent. `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/tooltip.tsx:15` and `hover-card.tsx:8` export Base UI roots directly with no local context for `open`, while `dialog.tsx`, `select.tsx`, `popover.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx`, and `lightbox.tsx` have custom roots; that means some roots will need new wrapper/context plumbing, not just content-level edits.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-1",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: The named WP3 file list misses supporting infrastructure for uncontrolled roots. For example, `src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TrackLabel.tsx:130-143` renders `<Dialog>` without an `open` prop and `src/domains/billing/components/CreditsManagement/components/TaskLogFilters.tsx:51-62` renders `<Popover>` uncontrolled, so the plan needs root-level state/context plumbing in addition to updating the 8 overlay content components.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-2",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: The same infrastructure gap exists for tooltip-like primitives. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/FinalVideoSectionControls.tsx:100-115` uses uncontrolled `<Tooltip>`, while `src/pages/Home/components/panes/CreativePartnerPane.tsx:109-126` uses controlled `<Tooltip open={...}>`; without a root wrapper that can serve both forms, changing only `TooltipContent` will not cover all real usages.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: The current caller contracts for overlay roots are mixed controlled and uncontrolled, so a plan that only drills a caller-supplied `open` prop is incomplete. `src/shared/components/ui/dialog.test.tsx:31-39` renders `<Dialog onOpenChange={...}>` with no `open`, `src/shared/components/ui/select.test.tsx:15-20` renders `<Select defaultValue=\"\">`, and the repo contains many uncontrolled `<Popover>` and `<Tooltip>` call sites, which means the callee-side wrappers need to source `open` themselves for some primitives.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-175",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the real callers of the safe timeline hooks in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that code passes through both mounted-editor values and the `null` outside-provider case, then uses `ops.registergenerationasset`, `ops.handleassetdrop`, and `data.resolvedconfig?.clips` when available, so any replacement api has to preserve both nullable access and the current immediate-drop contract rather than only exposing non-null store selectors.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-176",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` as a caller of `usetimelineeditordata()`. it only reads `dataref`, but it passes that mutable ref straight into `useclipresizegesture`, which relies on latest timeline data during pointer sequences; the plan does not yet say how this exact caller will receive a stable mutable adapter once the context is removed.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },

      "id": "DEBT-177",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/videoeditorshell.tsx`, where `handlekeyboarddelete` combines `editordata.dataref.current` with `editordata.selectedclipids` to build a mutation before falling back to `handledeleteclips`. that caller needs synchronized latest-ref and latest-selection semantics, so splitting those reads across unrelated store selectors without an adapter can change behavior even if each field is individually correct.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",

        "occurrence_count": 1,

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
